LEVEL|1|Everyday Nouns & Copula (-이에요/-예요)|마을 광장 (Town Square)|9|9
SUMMARY|Inspect everyday objects and meet locals to practice core nouns and stating what things are.
TIP|Attach -이에요 after a consonant (받침): 책이에요.
TIP|Attach -예요 after a vowel: 커피예요.
TIP|Korean places the verb/copula at the very end.
VOCAB|커피=coffee;책=book;사과=apple;우산=umbrella;학생=student;친구=friend
MAP|TTTTTTTTTTTTTTTTTT
MAP|TBBBBTT....TTRRRRT
MAP|TWWWWTT....TTWWWWT
MAP|TFFFF..PPPP..FFFFT
MAP|TFCFF..PPPP..FFCRT
MAP|T..f...PPPP...f..T
MAP|T......PPPP......T
MAP|TPPPPPPPPPPPPPPPPT
MAP|T..f...PPPP...f..T
MAP|T......PPPP......T
MAP|T~~....PPPP....~~T
MAP|TTTTTTTTTTTTTTTTTT
ENTITY|l1_e1|Table Item|objects/coffee|3|3|A warm cup sits on the cafe counter.
TASK|choice|이거 뭐예요? (What is this?)|커피예요;책이에요;우산이에요;사과예요|커피예요|커피 ends in a vowel, so it takes -예요.
TASK|fill|이거는 커피___.|예요;이에요;은;을|예요|No 받침 on 커피 -> -예요.
ENTITY|l1_e2|Blue Item|objects/book|14|3|Someone left a book on the bench.
TASK|choice|이거 뭐예요? (What is this?)|책이에요;책예요;물이에요;사과예요|책이에요|책 ends in ㄱ (받침) -> -이에요.
TASK|build|Build: This is a book.|이거는;책이에요|커피예요;책예요|Topic 이거는 + noun + copula 책이에요.
ENTITY|l1_e3|Minji (민지)|sprites/npc_barista|5|8|안녕하세요! 카페에 오신 것을 환영해요.

ENTITY|l1_e4|Lost Umbrella|objects/umbrella|12|8|A pink umbrella leans by the path.
TASK|choice|저거 뭐예요?|우산이에요;우산예요;사과예요;커피예요|우산이에요|우산 ends in ㄴ -> -이에요.
TASK|build|Build: That over there is an umbrella. [이다]|저거는;우산이에요|우산예요;사과예요|우산 ends in ㄴ -> -이에요.
ENDLEVEL
LEVEL|2|Demonstratives (이/그/저) & Questions|동네 골목 (Neighborhood Alley)|9|10
SUMMARY|Point out items near you, near the listener, or far away using 이, 그, 저.
TIP|이 = this (near speaker), 그 = that (near listener), 저 = that over there (far from both).
TIP|이것/이거 = this thing, 여기/거기/저기 = here/there/over there.
TIP|누구예요? = Who is it? 어디예요? = Where is it?
VOCAB|이거=this thing;그거=that thing;저거=that over there;여기=here;어디=where;누구=who

MAP|T..f...PPPP...f..T

LEVEL|3|Existence & Possession (있다 / 없다)|편의점 앞 (Convenience Store)|9|10
SUMMARY|Learn to say what exists, what you have, and what is missing using 있어요 and 없어요.
TIP|있어요 means 'exists / has'; 없어요 means 'does not exist / does not have'.
TIP|Always pair 있다/없다 with the subject particle 이/가 (시간이 있어요).
TIP|Location of existence uses 에 (집에 있어요 = is at home).
VOCAB|있다=to exist/have;없다=to not exist/have;시간=time;돈=money;가방=bag;집=home

LEVEL|4|Topic (은/는) vs Subject (이/가)|중앙 공원 (Central Park)|9|10
SUMMARY|Master the difference between topic markers 은/는 and subject markers 이/가.
TIP|Consonant ending (받침): 은 / 이. Vowel ending: 는 / 가.
TIP|은/는 introduces a topic or shows contrast (사과는 싸요 = As for apples, they're cheap).
TIP|이/가 spotlights new information or answers 'who/what'.
VOCAB|날씨=weather;오늘=today;이름=name;한국=Korea;공원=park;음식=food

MAP|T......PPPP......T
MAP|T......PPPP......T
MAP|TTTTTTTTTTTTTTTTTT

LEVEL|5|Polite Present Adjectives (-아요/-어요)|과일 시장 (Fruit Market)|9|10
SUMMARY|Conjugate descriptive verbs (adjectives) into polite present tense using vowel harmony.
TIP|Stem last vowel ㅏ or ㅗ -> add -아요 (좋다 -> 좋아요, 싸다 -> 싸요).
TIP|All other vowels -> add -어요 (맛있다 -> 맛있어요, 넓다 -> 넓어요).
TIP|하다 adjectives always become 해요 (조용하다 -> 조용해요).
VOCAB|맛있다=delicious;비싸다=expensive;싸다=cheap;작다=small;좋다=good;조용하다=quiet
MAP|TTTTTTTTTTTTTTTTTT
MAP|TRRRT..PPPP..TBBBT
MAP|TWWWT..PPPP..TWWWT
MAP|TFFFT..PPPP..TFFFT
MAP|T...PPPPPPPPPP...T
MAP|T...P..f..f..P...T
MAP|T...PPPPPPPPPP...T
MAP|TBBBT..PPPP..TRRRT
MAP|TWWWT..PPPP..TWWWT
MAP|TFFFT..PPPP..TFFFT
MAP|T......PPPP......T
MAP|TTTTTTTTTTTTTTTTTT
ENTITY|l5_e1|Vendor (아저씨)|sprites/npc_vendor|2|3|신선한 과일 보고 가세요!

TASK|build|Build: Apples are cheap. [싸다]|사과가;싸요|사과이;싸어요|사과 + 가 + 싸요.

TASK|build|Build: The book is small. [작다]|책이;작아요|작어요;책가|작다 has vowel ㅏ -> 작아요.

LEVEL|6|Action Verbs & Object Particle (을/를)|한식당 (Korean Restaurant)|9|10
SUMMARY|Describe everyday actions and mark direct objects properly with 을 and 를.
TIP|Consonant ending -> 을 (밥을 먹어요). Vowel ending -> 를 (커피를 마셔요).
TIP|Vowel contractions: 보 + 아요 -> 봐요, 마시 + 어요 -> 마셔요, 가 + 아요 -> 가요.
TIP|Word order: Subject + Object + Verb.
VOCAB|먹다=to eat;마시다=to drink;읽다=to read;보다=to watch/see;사다=to buy;밥=rice/meal

MAP|T..PPPPPPPPPPPP..T
MAP|T..P..f....f..P..T
MAP|T..P..........P..T
MAP|T..PPPP....PPPP..T
MAP|T......PPPP......T
MAP|T..f...PPPP...f..T
MAP|T......PPPP......T
MAP|TTTTTTTTTTTTTTTTTT

LEVEL|7|하다 Verbs & Daily Routines|대학교 캠퍼스 (University Campus)|9|9
SUMMARY|Master noun+하다 action verbs to talk about studying, working, and exercising.
TIP|Many Korean verbs are [Noun] + 하다: 공부하다 (study), 운동하다 (exercise), 일하다 (work).
TIP|하다 always conjugates to 해요 in polite present tense.
TIP|You can split them with 을/를: 한국어를 공부해요 or 공부를 해요.
VOCAB|공부하다=to study;운동하다=to exercise;일하다=to work;요리하다=to cook;전화하다=to call;한국어=Korean

LEVEL|8|Locations: 에 (Destination) vs 에서 (Action)|도서관 거리 (Library Street)|9|10
SUMMARY|Stop mixing up 에 and 에서! Learn when to use each location particle.
TIP|Use 에 for destinations (가다/오다) and static existence (있다/없다): 학교에 가요.
TIP|Use 에서 where an active action takes place: 도서관에서 책을 읽어요.
TIP|에서 also means 'from' when paired with 오다: 스웨덴에서 왔어요.
VOCAB|도서관=library;학교=school;카페=cafe;식당=restaurant;가다=to go;오다=to come
MAP|TTTTTTTTTTTTTTTTTT
MAP|T..f...~~~~...f..T
MAP|T......~~~~......T
MAP|T..PPPPPPPPPPPP..T
MAP|T..P..f....f..P..T
MAP|TRRP..........PBPT
MAP|TWWP..........PWWT
MAP|TFFPPPPPPPPPPPPFFT
MAP|T......PPPP......T
MAP|T..f...PPPP...f..T
MAP|T......PPPP......T
MAP|TTTTTTTTTTTTTTTTTT
ENTITY|l8_e1|Librarian (사서)|sprites/npc_elder|2|7|도서관에서는 조용히 해 주세요.

LEVEL|11|Negation: 안 & -지 않아요|카페 테라스 (Cafe Terrace)|9|10
SUMMARY|Say what you do not do or what something is not like using 안 and -지 않아요.

LEVEL|15|Progressive Tense (-고 있어요)|홍대 거리 (Hongdae Street)|9|10

TIP|Often paired with 지금 (now).

LEVEL|20|Irregular Verbs I: ㅂ & ㅡ Irregulars|계절 공원 (Seasons Park)|9|10
SUMMARY|Conquer the two most common Korean irregular patterns: ㅂ and ㅡ stems.
TIP|ㅂ irregular: drop ㅂ, add 우 + 어요 -> 워요 (덥다 -> 더워요, 춥다 -> 추워요, 쉽다 -> 쉬워요). Exception: 돕다 -> 도와요.
TIP|ㅡ irregular: drop ㅡ and look at the vowel before it (바쁘다 -> 바빠요, 예쁘다 -> 예뻐요, 쓰다 -> 써요).
TIP|Regular ㅂ verbs like 입다 (wear) and 잡다 (catch) do NOT change (입어요).
VOCAB|덥다=hot;춥다=cold;어렵다=difficult;쉽다=easy;예쁘다=pretty;쓰다=to write/use

LEVEL|21|Irregular Verbs II: ㄷ & ㄹ Irregulars|음악 거리 (Music Street)|9|10
SUMMARY|Master ㄷ-irregular verbs (듣다, 걷다, 묻다) and ㄹ-dropping verbs (살다, 알다, 만들다).
TIP|ㄷ irregular: before a vowel, ㄷ turns into ㄹ (듣다 -> 들어요, 걷다 -> 걸어요, 묻다 -> 물어요).
TIP|Regular ㄷ verbs like 닫다 (close) and 받다 (receive) stay ㄷ (닫아요, 받아요).
TIP|ㄹ irregular: ㄹ drops before ㄴ, ㅂ, ㅅ (살다 -> 사세요, 만들다 -> 만드세요, 알다 -> 아세요).

LEVEL|22|Irregular Verbs III: 르 & ㅅ Irregulars|한강 공원 (Han River Park)|9|10
SUMMARY|Learn how 르 stems double ㄹ (모르다 -> 몰라요) and ㅅ stems drop ㅅ (낫다 -> 나아요).
TIP|르 irregular: drop ㅡ, add an extra ㄹ to the previous syllable + 아/어요 (모르다 -> 몰라요, 빠르다 -> 빨라요, 부르다 -> 불러요).
TIP|ㅅ irregular: drop ㅅ before a vowel, do NOT contract the vowels (낫다 -> 나아요, 짓다 -> 지어요).
TIP|Regular ㅅ verbs like 웃다 (laugh) and 씻다 (wash) keep ㅅ (웃어요, 씻어요).
VOCAB|모르다=to not know;빠르다=fast;다르다=different;부르다=to sing/call;낫다=to recover/better;짓다=to build

MAP|T..PPPP....PPPP..T

LEVEL|28|Sequence (-고 나서) & Simultaneous Actions (-(으)면서)|북카페 (Book Cafe)|9|10
SUMMARY|Express 'after finishing A, do B' (-고 나서) and 'while doing A, do B' (-(으)면서).
TIP|-고 나서 emphasizes completing the first action before starting the next: 숙제를 하고 나서 놀아요.
TIP|-(으)면서 means doing two things at the same time (same subject!): 음악을 들으면서 공부해요.
TIP|Vowel/ㄹ stem -> -면서; consonant stem -> -으면서 (먹으면서).

LEVEL|31|Present Noun Modifiers (-는 + Noun)|서점 거리 (Bookstore Street)|9|9
SUMMARY|Turn action verbs into relative clauses before a noun using -는 (the X that is doing / does).
TIP|Attach -는 to ANY action verb stem to modify a following noun: 먹는 음식 (food that I eat), 좋아하는 노래 (song that I like).
TIP|ㄹ-stem verbs drop ㄹ before -는: 살다 -> 사는 곳 (the place where I live), 만들다 -> 만드는 음식.
TIP|Note: 있다/없다 also take -는 (맛있는 음식, 재미있는 책).

LEVEL|35|Reasons with -(으)니까 vs -아서/어서|버스 터미널 (Bus Terminal)|9|10
SUMMARY|Learn when to use -(으)니까 for reasons — especially before commands and suggestions!
TIP|Use -(으)니까 before imperatives (-(으)세요) and suggestions (-(으)ㅂ시다, -(으)ㄹ까요?) where -아서/어서 is forbidden!
TIP|Example: 추우니까 옷을 입으세요 (Since it's cold, put on a coat).
TIP|Vowel/ㄹ -> -니까 (가니까, 사니까); consonant -> -으니까 (먹으니까, 추우니까).
VOCAB|춥다=cold;늦다=late;우산=umbrella;챙기다=pack/bring;서두르다=hurry;피곤하다=tired

LEVEL|41|Indirect Speech I: Statements (-다고 하다)|신문사 앞 (Newspaper Office)|9|10
SUMMARY|Report what someone else said using indirect quotation (-다고 하다 / -(으)ㄴ다고 하다).
TIP|Action verbs (present): consonant + -는다고 해요 (먹는다고 해요), vowel/ㄹ + -ㄴ다고 해요 (간다고 해요).
TIP|Adjectives (present): stem + -다고 해요 (맛있다고 해요, 바쁘다고 해요).
TIP|Past tense (all): -았/었다고 해요 (갔다고 해요). Nouns: -(이)라고 해요.
VOCAB|말하다=say;듣다=hear;소식=news;바쁘다=busy;가다=go;학생=student

LEVEL|42|Indirect Speech II: Questions & Requests (-냐고 / -(으)라고)|고객 센터 (Service Center)|9|10
SUMMARY|Quote questions with -냐고 하다 and commands/requests with -(으)라고 하다.
TIP|Questions: -(느/으)냐고 해요 -> in modern speech, simply stem + -냐고 해요 (어디 가냐고 해요, 맛있냐고 해요).
TIP|Commands/Requests: stem + -(으)라고 해요 (빨리 오라고 해요, 책을 읽으라고 해요).
TIP|Suggestions ('Let's'): stem + -자고 해요 (같이 먹자고 해요).
VOCAB|물어보다=ask;부탁하다=request;기다리다=wait;빨리=quickly;같이=together;조용히=quietly

LEVEL|43|Guessing & Supposition (-(으)ㄴ/는 것 같다)|기상청 앞 (Weather Station)|9|9

VOCAB|아마=probably;눈이 오다=snow;늦다=be late;맛있다=delicious;비싸다=expensive;좋다=good

LEVEL|46|Passive Verbs (-이/히/리/기- & -아/어지다)|미술관 (Art Museum)|9|10
SUMMARY|Understand how Korean expresses passive states (opened, heard, seen, became).
TIP|Lexical passives use -이/히/리/기-: 보다->보이다 (be seen), 듣다->들리다 (be heard), 열다->열리다 (open), 닫다->닫히다 (close).
TIP|하다 verbs often switch to 되다: 준비하다 -> 준비되다 (be prepared), 시작하다 -> 시작되다.
TIP|-아/어지다 expresses 'become [Adj]' or passive state: 좋아지다 (become better), 만들어지다 (be made).
VOCAB|보이다=be seen;들리다=be heard;열리다=be opened;닫히다=be closed;준비되다=be ready;좋아지다=become better

LEVEL|47|Causative Verbs (-게 하다 & -이/히/리/기/우-)|요리 교실 (Cooking Class)|9|10
SUMMARY|Express 'making/letting someone do something' using -게 하다 and causative stems.
TIP|Universal causative: Verb/Adj stem + -게 하다 (먹게 해요 = let/make eat, 웃게 해요 = make laugh).
TIP|Short lexical causatives: 먹다->먹이다 (feed), 입다->입히다 (dress), 자다->재우다 (put to sleep), 울다->울리다 (make cry).
TIP|The person being made to act takes 에게/한테 or 을/를.
VOCAB|웃게 하다=make laugh;먹이다=to feed;입히다=to dress;재우다=put to sleep;알리다=inform;기다리다=wait

MAP|TWWWT..PPPP..TWWWT

MAP|T...PPPPPPPPPP...T
MAP|T...P..f..f..P...T
MAP|T...PPPPPPPPPP...T
MAP|TBBBT..PPPP..TRRRT
MAP|TWWWT..PPPP..TWWWT
MAP|TFFFT..PPPP..TFFFT
MAP|T......PPPP......T
MAP|TTTTTTTTTTTTTTTTTT

LEVEL|48|Concession: Even Though (-아/어도 & -(으)ㄹ지라도)|남산 성곽길 (Seoul City Wall)|9|10

LEVEL|49|Change of State (-게 되다) & Past Habits (-곤 하다)|추억의 거리 (Memory Lane)|9|9
SUMMARY|Talk about how things turned out (-게 되다) and things you used to do often (-곤 하다).
TIP|-게 되다 = ended up doing / came to be (often due to circumstances): 한국에서 살게 되었어요 (I ended up living in Korea).
TIP|-곤 하다 = used to / often do as a habit: 어릴 때 공원에서 놀곤 했어요 (When young, I used to play in the park).
TIP|Both attach cleanly to the verb stem with no irregular vowel changes!
VOCAB|알게 되다=come to know;살게 되다=end up living;자주=often;어릴 때=when young;추억=memory;습관=habit

LEVEL|51|Ch.2-1: Present Tense Endings|강남역 사거리 (Gangnam Station)|8|10
SUMMARY|Master all 4 Korean present tense registers: polite (-아/어요), formal (-(스)ㅂ니다), casual (-아/어), and plain (-는다).
TIP|-아/어요: Polite present (가요, 먹어요).
TIP|-(스)ㅂ니다: Formal present (갑니다, 먹습니다). ㄹ drops before ㅂ (만듭니다).
TIP|-아/어: Casual present (가, 먹어). -는다/ㄴ다: Plain present (간다, 먹는다).
VOCAB|가요=go (polite);갑니다=go (formal);가=go (casual);간다=go (plain)

MAP|KNNNNK..AA..KNNNNK

LEVEL|53|Ch.2-3: Future Tense Endings|삼성동 코엑스 (COEX Mall)|9|10
SUMMARY|Distinguish polite future (-(으)ㄹ 거예요), formal future (-(으)ㄹ 겁니다), promises (-(으)ㄹ게요), and conjecture (-겠어요).

TIP|-(으)ㄹ게요: First-person promise/intention (갈게요, 먹을게요).
TIP|-겠어요: Polite conjecture or strong determination (가겠어요, 먹겠어요).

LEVEL|54|Ch.2-4: Progressive & Continuous|청계천 광장 (Cheonggyecheon)|9|10
SUMMARY|Express ongoing actions (-고 있다), resulting states (-아/어 있다), gradual change (-아/어 가다/오다), and becoming (-게 되다).
TIP|-고 있다: Progressive action in progress (가고 있어요, 먹고 있어요).
TIP|-아/어 있다: Resulting state (앉아 있어요). -아/어 가다/오다: Gradual progress forward/to present.
TIP|-게 되다: Come to / get to (알게 됐어요, 살게 됐어요).
VOCAB|가고 있어요=going;앉아 있어요=sitting;배워 왔어요=have been learning;알게 됐어요=came to know

MAP|KSSSSSAA..AASSSSSK
MAP|KSSSSSAAAAAAXSSSSK
MAP|KGGGK.SSLLSS.KGGGK
MAP|KNNNK.SSLLSS.KNNNK
MAP|KSSSSSSSLLSSSSSSSK
MAP|KSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSK
MAP|KKKKKKKKKKKKKKKKKK

LEVEL|55|Ch.2-5: Sequential Actions|광화문 세종대로 (Sejong-daero)|8|10
SUMMARY|Chain events in exact chronological order using -고, -아/어서, -(으)ㄴ 후에, -기 전에, and -자마자.
TIP|-고: And then (먹고 자요). -아/어서: After/because (먹어서 배불러요).
TIP|-(으)ㄴ 후에: After (먹은 후에). -기 전에: Before (먹기 전에).
TIP|-자마자: As soon as (먹자마자).

LEVEL|56|Ch.2-6: Simultaneous Actions|명동 중앙로 (Myeongdong Central)|9|10
SUMMARY|Contrast doing two things at once (-(으)면서) with interrupting/shifting mid-action (-다가).
TIP|-(으)면서: While doing A, also do B (먹으면서 봐요). Same subject required.
TIP|-다가: While doing A, then shifted/interrupted to B (먹다가 나갔어요).
TIP|Watch ㄷ-irregulars before -(으)면서: 듣다 -> 들으면서.
VOCAB|먹으면서=while eating;먹다가=while eating, then;들으면서=while listening

LEVEL|57|Ch.2-7: Contrast Endings|종로 3가 (Jongno 3-ga)|9|10
SUMMARY|Contrast ideas directly with -지만 or smoothly with contextual -는데 / -(으)ㄴ데.
TIP|-지만: Direct 'but' (먹지만 배고파요).
TIP|-는데: Action verb context / soft 'but' (먹는데 맛없어요).
TIP|-(으)ㄴ/는데: Sets background before a question or proposal (가는데 같이 갈래요?).
VOCAB|먹지만=eat, but;먹는데=eating, and/but;가는데=going, and...

LEVEL|61|Ch.2-11: Ability & Possibility|성수동 카페거리 (Seongsu-dong)|9|10
SUMMARY|Express what you can do (-(으)ㄹ 수 있다), cannot do (-(으)ㄹ 수 없다), and circumstantial inability (-지 못하다).
TIP|-(으)ㄹ 수 있다: Can / is possible (갈 수 있어요, 먹을 수 있어요).
TIP|-(으)ㄹ 수 없다: Cannot / is impossible (갈 수 없어요).
TIP|-지 못하다: Circumstantial cannot (가지 못해요).
VOCAB|갈 수 있어요=can go;갈 수 없어요=cannot go;가지 못해요=cannot go (circumstance)
MAP|KKKKKKKKKKKKKKKKKK
MAP|KGGGGKSSSSSSKGGGGK
MAP|KGGGGKSLLLLSKGGGGK
MAP|KNNNNKSLLLLSKNNNNK
MAP|KSSSSSSLLLLSSSSSSK
MAP|KAAAAAAXXXXAAAAAAK
MAP|KSSSSSSLLLLSSSSSSK
MAP|KSSLLSSSSSSSSLLSSK
MAP|KSSLLSS.TT.SSLLSSK
MAP|KNNNNSSSSSSSSNNNNK
MAP|KSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSK
MAP|KKKKKKKKKKKKKKKKKK
ENTITY|l61_e1|Gangnam Commuter (직장인)|sprites/npc_vendor|3|4|배고픈데 떡볶이 먹고 싶어요!
TASK|choice|Conjugate 읽다 (to read) into Can (-(으)ㄹ 수 있어요):|읽을 수 있어요;읽을 수 없어요;읽지 못해요;읽어요|읽을 수 있어요|읽다 -> 읽을 수 있어요 (can).
TASK|fill|오늘은 ___. (Cannot (-(으)ㄹ 수 없어요)) [듣다 - to listen (ㄷ-irreg)]|들을 수 없어요;들을 수 있어요;듣지 못해요;듣지 않아요|들을 수 없어요|듣다 -> 들을 수 없어요 (cannot).
TASK|build|Build (Circumstantial cannot (-지 못해요)): 바빠서 + 만들다 (to make (ㄹ-irreg))|바빠서;만들지;못해요|만들 수 있어요;만들지 않아요|만들다 -> 만들지 못해요 (cannot due to circumstance).
TASK|choice|Conjugate 쓰다 (to write (ㅡ-irreg)) into Can (-(으)ㄹ 수 있어요):|쓸 수 있어요;쓸 수 없어요;쓰지 못해요;써요|쓸 수 있어요|쓰다 -> 쓸 수 있어요 (can).
TASK|fill|오늘은 ___. (Cannot (-(으)ㄹ 수 없어요)) [가다 - to go]|갈 수 없어요;갈 수 있어요;가지 못해요;가지 않아요|갈 수 없어요|가다 -> 갈 수 없어요 (cannot).

LEVEL|62|Ch.2-12: Obligation & Necessity|여의도 국회의사당 (National Assembly)|9|10
SUMMARY|Master 4 ways to express duty and necessity: -아/어야 하다, -아/어야 되다, -(으)ㄹ 필요가 있다, and -아/어야지.
TIP|-아/어야 하다: Must (가야 해요). -아/어야 되다: Should/must (가야 돼요).
TIP|-(으)ㄹ 필요가 있다: Need to (갈 필요가 있어요).
TIP|-아/어야지: Self-reminder 'should/must' (가야지).
VOCAB|가야 해요=must go;가야 돼요=should go;갈 필요가 있어요=need to go;가야지=gotta go

MAP|KSSSSSAA..AASSSSSK
MAP|KSSSSSAAAAAAXSSSSK
MAP|KGGGK.SSLLSS.KGGGK
MAP|KNNNK.SSLLSS.KNNNK
MAP|KSSSSSSSLLSSSSSSSK
MAP|KSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSK
MAP|KKKKKKKKKKKKKKKKKK

TASK|build|Build (Should (-아/어야 돼요)): 꼭 + 가다 (to go)|꼭;가야;돼요|가야 해요;갈 필요가 있어요|가다 -> 가야 돼요 (should).
TASK|choice|Conjugate 먹다 (to eat) into Need to (-(으)ㄹ 필요가 있어요):|먹을 필요가 있어요;먹어야 해요;먹어야 돼요;먹어야지|먹을 필요가 있어요|먹다 -> 먹을 필요가 있어요 (need to).
TASK|fill|이제 ___. (Self-reminder (-아/어야지)) [보다 - to see/watch]|봐야지;봐야 해요;봐야 돼요;볼 필요가 있어요|봐야지|보다 -> 봐야지 (should/must).

LEVEL|63|Ch.2-13: Permission & Prohibition|시청 앞 광장 (Seoul City Hall)|8|10
SUMMARY|Ask permission (-아/어도 되다, -(으)ㄹ 수 있다) and state prohibitions (-(으)면 안 되다, -지 마세요).
TIP|-아/어도 되다: May / is allowed (가도 돼요, 먹어도 돼요).
TIP|-(으)면 안 되다: Must not (가면 안 돼요, 먹으면 안 돼요).
TIP|-지 마세요: Please don't (가지 마세요). -(으)ㄹ 수 있다: Can/may.
VOCAB|가도 돼요=may go;가면 안 돼요=must not go;가지 마세요=don't go

MAP|KSSLLL..AA..LLLSSK
MAP|KSSSSSSSAASSSSSSSK
MAP|K..T..SSXXSS..T..K
MAP|KSSSSSSSAASSSSSSSK
MAP|KNNNNK..AA..KNNNNK
MAP|KSSSSS..XX..SSSSSK
MAP|KKKKKKKKKKKKKKKKKK

LEVEL|65|Ch.2-15: Requests & Suggestions|신촌 연세로 (Sinchon)|9|10
SUMMARY|Practice all 5 request & suggestion forms: -아/어 주세요, -(으)세요, -(으)ㄹ까요?, -자, and -지 않을래요?.
TIP|-아/어 주세요: Please do for me (가 주세요). -(으)세요: Honorific please (가세요).
TIP|-(으)ㄹ까요?: Shall we? (갈까요?). -자: Casual 'Let's' (가자).
TIP|-지 않을래요?: Polite 'Won't you...?' (가지 않을래요?).
VOCAB|가 주세요=please go;가세요=please go (hon);갈까요?=shall we?;가자=let's go

MAP|KGGGGKSSSSSSKGGGGK

MAP|KSSSSSSLLLLSSSSSSK
MAP|KSSLLSSSSSSSSLLSSK
MAP|KSSLLSS.TT.SSLLSSK

MAP|KSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSK

ENTITY|l65_e1|Gangnam Commuter (직장인)|sprites/npc_vendor|3|4|배고픈데 떡볶이 먹고 싶어요!

TASK|fill|여기로 ___. (Honorific please (-(으)세요)) [먹다 - to eat]|드세요;먹어 주세요;먹을까요?;먹자|드세요|먹다 -> 드세요 (honorific please).
TASK|build|Build (Shall we? (-(으)ㄹ까요?)): 같이 + 보다 (to see/watch)|같이;볼까요?|봐 주세요;보세요|보다 -> 볼까요? (shall we?).
TASK|choice|Conjugate 읽다 (to read) into Casual 'Let's' (-자):|읽자;읽을까요?;읽어 주세요;읽지 않을래요?|읽자|읽다 -> 읽자 (casual let's).
TASK|fill|같이 ___. (Won't you? (-지 않을래요?)) [듣다 - to listen (ㄷ-irreg)]|듣지 않을래요?;들을까요?;들어 주세요;듣지 마세요|듣지 않을래요?|듣다 -> 듣지 않을래요? (won't you?).

LEVEL|66|Ch.2-16: Giving & Receiving Actions|이태원 거리 (Itaewon)|9|10
SUMMARY|Show who benefits from an action using -아/어 주다, -아/어 드리다 (humble/honorific), and -아/어 받다.
TIP|-아/어 주다: Do something for someone (가 줘요, 만들어 줘요).
TIP|-아/어 드리다: Do something for an elder/superior (가 드려요, 읽어 드려요).
TIP|-아/어 받다: Receive the benefit of an action (가 받아요).
VOCAB|가 줘요=do for someone;가 드려요=do for elder;가 받아요=receive action

MAP|KSSSSSSSLLSSSSSSSK
MAP|KSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSK
MAP|KKKKKKKKKKKKKKKKKK

LEVEL|68|Ch.2-18: Completion & Preparation|동대문 디자인플라자 (DDP)|9|10
SUMMARY|Express completely finishing (-아/어 버리다), regrettably ending up doing (-고 말다), and doing in advance (-아/어 놓다).
TIP|-아/어 버리다: Completely do (often with relief or regret: 가 버렸어요, 먹어 버렸어요).
TIP|-고 말다: End up doing (가고 말았어요).
TIP|-아/어 놓다 / 두다: Do in advance and leave it ready (만들어 놨어요).
VOCAB|가 버렸어요=completely left;가고 말았어요=ended up going;만들어 놨어요=made in advance

MAP|KSSAA..~~~~..AASSK
MAP|KSSAAXXAAAAXXAASSK
MAP|KSSSSSSLLLLSSSSSSK
MAP|KNNNNKSSLLSSKNNNNK
MAP|KSSSSSSLLLLSSSSSSK
MAP|KKKKKKKKKKKKKKKKKK

LEVEL|69|Ch.2-19: All Negative Forms|압구정 로데오 (Apgujeong)|9|10
SUMMARY|Review and contrast all 5 negative structures: 안, -지 않다, 못, -지 못하다, and -지 마세요.
TIP|안 + Verb / -지 않다: Simple 'not' (안 가요, 가지 않아요).
TIP|못 + Verb / -지 못하다: 'Cannot' (못 가요, 가지 못해요).
TIP|-지 마세요: Imperative 'Please don't' (가지 마세요).
VOCAB|안 가요=don't go;가지 않아요=don't go;못 가요=can't go;가지 못해요=can't go;가지 마세요=don't go!
MAP|KKKKKKKKKKKKKKKKKK
MAP|KGGGGKSSSSSSKGGGGK
MAP|KGGGGKSLLLLSKGGGGK
MAP|KNNNNKSLLLLSKNNNNK
MAP|KSSSSSSLLLLSSSSSSK
MAP|KAAAAAAXXXXAAAAAAK
MAP|KSSSSSSLLLLSSSSSSK
MAP|KSSLLSSSSSSSSLLSSK
MAP|KSSLLSS.TT.SSLLSSK
MAP|KNNNNSSSSSSSSNNNNK
MAP|KSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSK
MAP|KKKKKKKKKKKKKKKKKK

TASK|fill|너무 바빠서 ___. (Short 'cannot' (못 + Verb)) [만들다 - to make (ㄹ-irreg)]|못 만들어요;안 만들어요;만들지 못해요;만들지 않아요|못 만들어요|만들다 -> 못 만들어요 (short inability).
TASK|build|Build (Long 'cannot' (-지 못해요)): 결국 + 쓰다 (to write (ㅡ-irreg))|결국;쓰지;못해요|못 써요;쓰지 않아요|쓰다 -> 쓰지 못해요 (long inability).
TASK|choice|Conjugate 가다 (to go) into Negative command (-지 마세요):|가지 마세요;가지 않아요;가지 못해요;가세요|가지 마세요|가다 -> 가지 마세요 (polite don't).
ENDLEVEL
LEVEL|70|Ch.2-20: Question Forms|가로수길 (Garosu-gil)|9|10
SUMMARY|Ask questions with exact nuance: simple (-아/어요?), shall we (-(으)ㄹ까요?), will you (-(으)ㄹ래요?), confirmation (-죠?), and reason (-(으)니까?).
TIP|-아/어요?: Simple polite question (가요?). -(으)ㄹ까요?: Shall we? (갈까요?).
TIP|-(으)ㄹ래요?: Will you / wanna? (갈래요?).
TIP|-지요? / -죠?: Right? Isn't it? (가죠?). -(으)니까?: Because? (가니까?).
VOCAB|가요?=go?;갈까요?=shall we?;갈래요?=will you?;가죠?=right?;가니까?=because?

LEVEL|71|Ch.2-21: Listing & Alternatives|익선동 한옥거리 (Ikseon-dong)|8|10
SUMMARY|Offer choices with -거나 (or) and express 'whether/or' indifference with -든지.
TIP|-거나: 'Or' between actions/states (먹거나 자요 = eat or sleep).
TIP|-든지: 'Whether ... or ...' (먹든지 말든지 = whether you eat or not).
TIP|Both attach directly to the verb stem (no irregular vowel shifts!).
VOCAB|먹거나=eat or;먹든지=whether eat;가거나=go or;가든지=whether go

MAP|KSSSSSSSAASSSSSSSK
MAP|KNNNNK..AA..KNNNNK
MAP|KSSSSS..XX..SSSSSK
MAP|KKKKKKKKKKKKKKKKKK

LEVEL|73|Ch.2-23: Nominal Forms (-기, -는 것, -(으)ㅁ)|서울도서관 (Seoul Library)|9|10
SUMMARY|Turn verbs into nouns using -기, -(으)ㄴ/는 것, and formal -(으)ㅁ/ㅁ.
TIP|-기: Noun form often used with 좋다/쉽다/어렵다 (가기 좋아요, 먹기 쉬워요).
TIP|-(으)ㄴ/는 것: Descriptive noun form (가는 것, 먹는 것).
TIP|-(으)ㅁ / ㅁ: Formal/written noun form (감, 먹음, 씀, 만듦).
VOCAB|가기=going;가는 것=the act of going;감=going (written);먹음=eating (written)
MAP|KKKKKKKKKKKKKKKKKK
MAP|KGGGGKSSSSSSKGGGGK
MAP|KGGGGKSLLLLSKGGGGK
MAP|KNNNNKSLLLLSKNNNNK
MAP|KSSSSSSLLLLSSSSSSK
MAP|KAAAAAAXXXXAAAAAAK
MAP|KSSSSSSLLLLSSSSSSK
MAP|KSSLLSSSSSSSSLLSSK
MAP|KSSLLSS.TT.SSLLSSK
MAP|KNNNNSSSSSSSSNNNNK
MAP|KSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSK
MAP|KKKKKKKKKKKKKKKKKK
ENTITY|l73_e1|Gangnam Commuter (직장인)|sprites/npc_vendor|3|4|배고픈데 떡볶이 먹고 싶어요!
TASK|choice|Conjugate 먹다 (to eat) into Noun form (-기):|먹기;먹는 것;먹음;먹어요|먹기|먹다 -> 먹기 (-기 nominalization).
TASK|fill|혼자 ___. (Descriptive noun form (-는 것)) [보다 - to see/watch]|보는 것;보기;봄;본 것|보는 것|보다 -> 보는 것 (-는 것 nominalization).
TASK|build|Build (Formal noun form (-(으)ㅁ/ㅁ)): 문서에 + 읽다 (to read)|문서에;읽음|읽기;읽는 것|읽다 -> 읽음 (formal -(으)ㅁ noun form).
TASK|choice|Conjugate 듣다 (to listen (ㄷ-irreg)) into Noun form (-기):|듣기;듣는 것;들음;들어요|듣기|듣다 -> 듣기 (-기 nominalization).
TASK|fill|혼자 ___. (Descriptive noun form (-는 것)) [만들다 - to make (ㄹ-irreg)]|만드는 것;만들기;만듦;만든 것|만드는 것|만들다 -> 만드는 것 (-는 것 nominalization).

LEVEL|74|Ch.2-24: Modifiers (Relative Clauses)|경복궁 앞 (Gyeongbokgung)|9|10
SUMMARY|Build relative clauses before nouns across all 3 tenses: -는 (present), -(으)ㄴ (past), and -(으)ㄹ (future).
TIP|-는 + Noun: Present modifier (가는 사람 = person who goes; 만드는 사람).
TIP|-(으)ㄴ + Noun: Past modifier (간 사람 = person who went; 먹은 사람).
TIP|-(으)ㄹ + Noun: Future modifier (갈 사람 = person who will go; 먹을 사람).
VOCAB|가는 사람=person going;간 사람=person who went;갈 사람=person who will go
MAP|KKKKKKKKKKKKKKKKKK
MAP|KNNNNK.SSSS.KNNNNK
MAP|KSSSSS.SLLS.SSSSSK
MAP|KSSLLXAAXXAAXLLSSK
MAP|KSSLLSAA..AASLSSSK
MAP|KSSSSSAA..AASSSSSK
MAP|KSSSSSAAAAAAXSSSSK
MAP|KGGGK.SSLLSS.KGGGK
MAP|KNNNK.SSLLSS.KNNNK
MAP|KSSSSSSSLLSSSSSSSK
MAP|KSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSK
MAP|KKKKKKKKKKKKKKKKKK
ENTITY|l74_e1|Gangnam Commuter (직장인)|sprites/npc_vendor|3|2|방금 버스 놓쳐서 다음 차 기다리는 중이에요.
TASK|choice|Conjugate 보다 (to see/watch) into Present modifier (-는 사람):|보는 사람;본 사람;볼 사람;봐요|보는 사람|보다 -> 보는 사람 (present modifier).
TASK|fill|어제 ___. (Past modifier (-(으)ㄴ 사람)) [읽다 - to read]|읽은 사람;읽는 사람;읽을 사람;읽었어요|읽은 사람|읽다 -> 읽은 사람 (past modifier).
TASK|build|Build (Future modifier (-(으)ㄹ 사람)): 내일 + 듣다 (to listen (ㄷ-irreg))|내일;들을;사람|듣는 사람;들은 사람|듣다 -> 들을 사람 (future modifier).
TASK|choice|Conjugate 만들다 (to make (ㄹ-irreg)) into Present modifier (-는 사람):|만드는 사람;만든 사람;만들 사람;만들어요|만드는 사람|만들다 -> 만드는 사람 (present modifier).
TASK|fill|어제 ___. (Past modifier (-(으)ㄴ 사람)) [쓰다 - to write (ㅡ-irreg)]|쓴 사람;쓰는 사람;쓸 사람;썼어요|쓴 사람|쓰다 -> 쓴 사람 (past modifier).

LEVEL|75|Ch.2-25: Emphasis & Intensification|잠실 롯데월드타워 (Jamsil Tower)|8|10
SUMMARY|Add nuance and emphasis using -기도 하다 (also/even), -기는 하다 (do, but...), and -(으)ㄴ/는데도 (even though).
TIP|-기도 하다: Also / even / sometimes do (가기도 해요, 먹기도 해요).
TIP|-기는 하다: Admit doing it, with a 'but' coming (가기는 해요).
TIP|-(으)ㄴ/는데도: Even though (가는데도, 먹는데도).
VOCAB|가기도 해요=also go;가기는 해요=do go, but;가는데도=even though going

LEVEL|76|Ch.2-26: Supposition & Appearance|남산 케이블카 (Namsan Cable Car)|9|10
SUMMARY|Express guesses and appearances with -(으)ㄴ/는 것 같다, -(으)ㄹ 것 같다, and -(으)ㄴ가 보다.
TIP|-(으)ㄴ/는 것 같다: Seems like (present/past: 가는 것 같아요).
TIP|-(으)ㄹ 것 같다: Will probably (future: 갈 것 같아요, 먹을 것 같아요).
TIP|-(으)ㄴ가 보다: Appears/seems based on observation (간가 봐요, 먹은가 봐요).
VOCAB|가는 것 같아요=seems to go;갈 것 같아요=will probably go;간가 봐요=appears they went

MAP|KSSSSSSLLLLSSSSSSK

LEVEL|77|Ch.2-27: Reason & Cause|신사역 교차로 (Sinsa Intersection)|9|10
SUMMARY|Distinguish neutral cause (-아/어서), speaker-judgment/command reason (-(으)니까), and negative-result preoccupation (-느라고).
TIP|-아/어서: Neutral cause/reason (가서 못 봤어요).
TIP|-(으)니까: Because/since — allows commands/suggestions (가니까 조심하세요).
TIP|-느라고: Because I was busy doing A, negative result B happened (가느라고 늦었어요).
VOCAB|가서=because go;가니까=since go;가느라고=because busy going
MAP|KKKKKKKKKKKKKKKKKK
MAP|KGGGGKSSSSSSKGGGGK
MAP|KGGGGKSLLLLSKGGGGK
MAP|KNNNNKSLLLLSKNNNNK
MAP|KSSSSSSLLLLSSSSSSK
MAP|KAAAAAAXXXXAAAAAAK
MAP|KSSSSSSLLLLSSSSSSK
MAP|KSSLLSSSSSSSSLLSSK
MAP|KSSLLSS.TT.SSLLSSK
MAP|KNNNNSSSSSSSSNNNNK
MAP|KSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSK
MAP|KKKKKKKKKKKKKKKKKK

LEVEL|78|Ch.2-28: Degree & Extent|코엑스 별마당도서관 (Starfield Library)|9|10
SUMMARY|Express manner (-게), extent/until (-도록), and 'enough to' (-(으)ㄹ 만큼).
TIP|-게: In a manner / so that (빨리 가게, 잘 보이게).
TIP|-도록: To the extent that / until (알도록, 밤새도록).
TIP|-(으)ㄹ 만큼: Enough to / as much as (갈 만큼, 먹을 만큼).
VOCAB|가게=so that;가도록=to the extent;갈 만큼=enough to go

LEVEL|79|Ch.2-29: Background & Context|광화문 야경 광장 (Gwanghwamun Night)|8|10
SUMMARY|Master the final Chapter 2 table: conversational background -는데 and explanatory 'you see' -거든요.
TIP|-는데: Sets conversational context or gentle contrast (가는데 같이 갈래요?).
TIP|-거든(요): Gives a reason/explanation the listener didn't know ('you see...': 바쁘거든요).
TIP|Combine everything you learned across all 79 levels to speak natural Seoul Korean!
VOCAB|가는데=going, and...;가거든요=go, you see;먹거든요=eat, you see
